{"parentUuid":"4260e1ab-b424-4e7b-836f-aec5bc7e5db4","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ae7ca5a3fc49dc061","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_015Yx1epzRWPuyYnQC6tbM9V","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_019gRQK3YmxDy4PPaYAkLDw9","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/tmp/create-stubs.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Parse orphan-stubs-needed.md, filter, categorize, and create stub pages.\"\"\"\n\nimport os\nimport re\n\nVAULT = \"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault\"\n\n# ---- Parse ----\nentries = []\nwith open(f\"{VAULT}/meta/orphan-stubs-needed.md\") as f:\n    for line in f:\n        m = re.match(r'^- \\*\\*(.*?)\\*\\* -- referenced in: (.*)$', line.strip())\n        if m:\n            entries.append((m.group(1).strip(), m.group(2).strip()))\n\nprint(f\"Total entries parsed: {len(entries)}\")\n\n# ---- Known D&D generic creatures/races/monster types to skip ----\nGENERIC_DND = {\n    # Races\n    \"Aasimar\", \"Bugbear\", \"Centaur\", \"Changeling\", \"Dhampir\", \"Dragonborn\",\n    \"Drow\", \"Dwarf\", \"Eladrin\", \"Elf\", \"Firbolg\", \"Genasi\", \"Githyanki\",\n    \"Githzerai\", \"Gnoll\", \"Gnome\", \"Goblin\", \"Goliath\", \"Grung\", \"Halfling\",\n    \"Half-Elf\", \"Half-Orc\", \"Harengon\", \"Hexblood\", \"Hobgoblin\", \"Human\",\n    \"Kalashtar\", \"Kenku\", \"Kobold\", \"Leonin\", \"Lizardfolk\", \"Loxodon\",\n    \"Minotaur\", \"Orc\", \"Owlin\", \"Reborn\", \"Satyr\", \"Shifter\", \"Tabaxi\",\n    \"Tiefling\", \"Tortle\", \"Triton\", \"Vedalken\", \"Warforged\", \"Yuan-Ti\",\n\n    # Common monster types / generic D&D creatures\n    \"Abolith\", \"Aboleth\", \"Abby Devil\", \"Abishai\", \"Ankheg\", \"Beholder\",\n    \"Bullywug\", \"Couatl\", \"Demilich\", \"Displacer Beast\", \"Doppelganger\",\n    \"Dracolich\", \"Drider\", \"Duergar\", \"Ettercap\", \"Ettin\",\n    \"Flameskull\", \"Flumph\", \"Galeb Duhr\", \"Gargoyle\", \"Gelatinous Cube\",\n    \"Ghost\", \"Ghoul\", \"Giant\", \"Gibbering Mouther\", \"Gith\", \"Golem\",\n    \"Gorgon\", \"Green Hag\", \"Grell\", \"Grick\", \"Griffon\", \"Grimlock\",\n    \"Guardian Naga\", \"Hag\", \"Harpy\", \"Hell Hound\", \"Hippogriff\",\n    \"Hobgoblin\", \"Homunculus\", \"Hook Horror\", \"Hydra\", \"Illithid\",\n    \"Imp\", \"Intellect Devourer\", \"Invisible Stalker\", \"Jackalwere\",\n    \"Kenku\", \"Kirin\", \"Kobold\", \"Kraken\", \"Kuo-Toa\",\n    \"Lamia\", \"Lich\", \"Lizardfolk\", \"Lycanthrope\", \"Manticore\",\n    \"Medusa\", \"Merfolk\", \"Merrow\", \"Mimic\", \"Mind Flayer\",\n    \"Mummy\", \"Myconid\", \"Naga\", \"Nightmare\", \"Nothic\",\n    \"Ogre\", \"Oni\", \"Otyugh\", \"Owlbear\", \"Pegasus\",\n    \"Peryton\", \"Phase Spider\", \"Pixie\", \"Pseudodragon\", \"Purple Worm\",\n    \"Quaggoth\", \"Rakshasa\", \"Remorhaz\", \"Revenant\", \"Roc\",\n    \"Roper\", \"Rust Monster\", \"Sahuagin\", \"Salamander\", \"Scarecrow\",\n    \"Shadow\", \"Shambling Mound\", \"Shield Guardian\", \"Skeleton\",\n    \"Slaad\", \"Specter\", \"Sphinx\", \"Sprite\", \"Stirge\",\n    \"Succubus\", \"Tarrasque\", \"Thri-Kreen\", \"Treant\", \"Troll\",\n    \"Umber Hulk\", \"Unicorn\", \"Vampire\", \"Vampire Spawn\",\n    \"Water Elemental\", \"Wight\", \"Will-o-Wisp\", \"Wraith\",\n    \"Wyvern\", \"Xorn\", \"Yeti\", \"Yuan-Ti\", \"Zombie\",\n\n    # Generic D&D terms / class features / spells\n    \"Paladin\", \"Warlock\", \"Wizard\", \"Sorcerer\", \"Cleric\", \"Ranger\",\n    \"Barbarian\", \"Bard\", \"Druid\", \"Fighter\", \"Monk\", \"Rogue\",\n    \"Artificer\", \"Blood Hunter\",\n\n    # Generic creature categories from npc-catalog context\n    \"Bearded Devil\", \"Barbed Devil\", \"Bone Devil\", \"Chain Devil\",\n    \"Erinyes\", \"Horned Devil\", \"Ice Devil\", \"Lemure\", \"Pit Fiend\",\n    \"Balor\", \"Dretch\", \"Glabrezu\", \"Hezrou\", \"Marilith\", \"Nalfeshnee\",\n    \"Quasit\", \"Vrock\", \"Yochlol\",\n    \"Death Knight\", \"Death Tyrant\", \"Demi-Lich\", \"Demogorgon\",\n    \"Elder Brain\", \"Empyrean\",\n    \"Fire Elemental\", \"Air Elemental\", \"Earth Elemental\",\n    \"Fire Giant\", \"Frost Giant\", \"Hill Giant\", \"Stone Giant\", \"Storm Giant\", \"Cloud Giant\",\n    \"Ancient Dragon\", \"Adult Dragon\", \"Young Dragon\", \"Dragon Turtle\",\n    \"Ancient Red Dragon\", \"Ancient Blue Dragon\", \"Ancient Green Dragon\",\n    \"Ancient Black Dragon\", \"Ancient White Dragon\",\n    \"Blink Dog\", \"Death Dog\", \"Winter Wolf\", \"Dire Wolf\",\n    \"Mummy Lord\", \"Vampire Lord\",\n}\n\n# Image extensions to skip\nIMAGE_EXTS = {'.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.gif', '.webp', '.webm', '.svg', '.bmp', '.tiff', '.mp4', '.mov'}\n\ndef is_image(name):\n    return any(name.lower().endswith(ext) for ext in IMAGE_EXTS)\n\ndef is_empty_or_malformed(name):\n    if not name or len(name.strip()) == 0:\n        return True\n    # Names that are just whitespace or single chars\n    if len(name.strip()) <= 1:\n        return True\n    # Names starting with space (like \" Desert\", \" Harbor\" etc - broken refs)\n    if name != name.strip():\n        # Check if the stripped version is also very generic\n        stripped = name.strip()\n        if stripped in (\"Desert\", \"Harbor\", \"Underground\", \"Volcano\", \"Forest\", \"Mountain\", \"River\", \"Ocean\", \"Cave\", \"Temple\", \"Castle\", \"Tower\", \"Village\", \"City\", \"Town\"):\n            return True\n    return False\n\ndef is_generic_dnd(name):\n    # Exact match\n    if name in GENERIC_DND:\n        return True\n    # Also check common patterns that indicate generic monsters\n    # e.g., \"Adult Red Dragon\", \"Young White Dragon\"\n    generic_prefixes = [\"Ancient \", \"Adult \", \"Young \", \"Wyrmling \"]\n    generic_suffixes = [\" Dragon\", \" Devil\", \" Demon\", \" Elemental\", \" Giant\", \" Zombie\", \" Skeleton\", \" Golem\"]\n    for prefix in generic_prefixes:\n        if name.startswith(prefix):\n            for suffix in generic_suffixes:\n                if name.endswith(suffix):\n                    return True\n    return False\n\ndef is_generic_single_word_location(name, ref):\n    \"\"\"Skip generic single-word location descriptors without proper nouns.\"\"\"\n    generics = {\"Desert\", \"Harbor\", \"Underground\", \"Volcano\", \"Forest\", \"Mountain\",\n                \"River\", \"Ocean\", \"Cave\", \"Temple\", \"Castle\", \"Tower\", \"Village\",\n                \"City\", \"Town\", \"Swamp\", \"Plains\", \"Ruins\", \"Dungeon\", \"Tavern\",\n                \"Market\", \"Arena\", \"Library\", \"Prison\", \"Graveyard\", \"Mine\"}\n    if name in generics:\n        return True\n    return False\n\n# ---- Categorize ----\ndef categorize(name, ref):\n    \"\"\"Determine type and output directory.\"\"\"\n    ref_lower = ref.lower()\n    name_lower = name.lower()\n\n    # Location indicators\n    location_keywords = [\"tower\", \"keep\", \"hall\", \"gate\", \"port\", \"district\", \"quarter\",\n                         \"ward\", \"square\", \"bridge\", \"market\", \"tavern\", \"inn\", \"temple\",\n                         \"shrine\", \"cathedral\", \"fortress\", \"citadel\", \"palace\", \"castle\",\n                         \"dungeon\", \"cave\", \"mine\", \"pit\", \"grove\", \"forest\", \"lake\",\n                         \"river\", \"mountain\", \"island\", \"bay\", \"harbor\", \"reef\",\n                         \"apothecary\", \"shop\", \"emporium\", \"bazaar\", \"arena\", \"colosseum\",\n                         \"library\", \"academy\", \"university\", \"school\", \"guildhall\",\n                         \"barracks\", \"prison\", \"graveyard\", \"cemetery\", \"docks\", \"wharf\",\n                         \"lighthouse\", \"observatory\", \"workshop\", \"foundry\", \"forge\",\n                         \"sanctum\", \"vault\", \"crypt\", \"tomb\", \"ruins\"]\n\n    faction_keywords = [\"guild\", \"order\", \"brotherhood\", \"sisterhood\", \"council\",\n                        \"alliance\", \"clan\", \"tribe\", \"syndicate\", \"cartel\", \"house\",\n                        \"court\", \"legion\", \"army\", \"circle\", \"coven\", \"cult\",\n                        \"church\", \"cloaks\", \"guard\", \"watch\", \"patrol\"]\n\n    item_keywords = [\"sword\", \"blade\", \"staff\", \"wand\", \"ring\", \"amulet\", \"necklace\",\n                     \"crown\", \"helm\", \"shield\", \"armor\", \"cloak\", \"boots\", \"gauntlets\",\n                     \"potion\", \"scroll\", \"tome\", \"book\", \"gem\", \"stone\", \"crystal\",\n                     \"orb\", \"rod\", \"scepter\", \"hammer\", \"axe\", \"bow\", \"dagger\",\n                     \"locket\", \"lantern\", \"horn\", \"flute\", \"lyre\", \"pendant\", \"key\",\n                     \"anchor\", \"heart\", \"diadem\", \"mask\"]\n\n    # Ref path hints\n    if \"locations/\" in ref_lower:\n        # Referenced from a location page - could be NPC in that location or sub-location\n        pass\n    if \"factions/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"faction\", f\"{VAULT}/factions\"\n\n    # Check name patterns\n    name_words = name_lower.split()\n\n    # Location patterns: \"The X\", \"X's Y\" where Y is a location word, multi-word place names\n    if any(name_lower.endswith(f\" {kw}\") or name_lower.endswith(f\"'s {kw}\") or f\" {kw} \" in name_lower for kw in location_keywords):\n        if \"cities/\" in ref_lower or \"city\" in name_lower:\n            return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/cities\"\n        if \"dungeons/\" in ref_lower or \"dungeon\" in name_lower:\n            return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/dungeons\"\n        if \"planes/\" in ref_lower or \"plane\" in name_lower:\n            return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/planes\"\n        if \"regions/\" in ref_lower:\n            return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/regions\"\n        return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations\"\n\n    # Faction patterns\n    if any(name_lower.endswith(f\" {kw}\") or f\" {kw} \" in name_lower or f\"'s {kw}\" in name_lower for kw in faction_keywords):\n        return \"faction\", f\"{VAULT}/factions\"\n    if name.startswith(\"The \") and any(kw in name_lower for kw in faction_keywords):\n        return \"faction\", f\"{VAULT}/factions\"\n\n    # Item patterns\n    if any(name_lower.endswith(f\" {kw}\") or f\" {kw} \" in name_lower for kw in item_keywords):\n        return \"item\", f\"{VAULT}/lore\"\n\n    # Plane/cosmology names referenced from planes\n    if \"planes/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/planes\"\n\n    # Specific location refs\n    if \"cities/\" in ref_lower:\n        # Could be NPC or location within a city\n        # Names with titles or personal names are likely NPCs\n        titles = [\"lord\", \"lady\", \"king\", \"queen\", \"prince\", \"princess\", \"duke\",\n                  \"baron\", \"count\", \"captain\", \"sergeant\", \"commander\", \"chief\",\n                  \"professor\", \"doctor\", \"master\", \"sir\", \"dame\", \"father\", \"mother\",\n                  \"brother\", \"sister\", \"elder\", \"ambassador\", \"general\", \"governor\",\n                  \"mayor\", \"sheriff\", \"warden\", \"priest\", \"priestess\", \"oracle\",\n                  \"seer\", \"sage\", \"mr\", \"mrs\", \"miss\", \"dr\"]\n        if any(name_lower.startswith(t + \" \") or name_lower.startswith(t + \".\") for t in titles):\n            return \"character\", f\"{VAULT}/characters/npcs/minor\"\n        # Multi-word names that look like person names (First Last)\n        if len(name_words) >= 2 and name_words[0][0].isupper() and name_words[-1][0].isupper():\n            # Could be NPC or location - check if it has location-like structure\n            if \"'\" in name and any(kw in name_lower for kw in location_keywords):\n                return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/cities\"\n            # Default: if referenced in city page, likely an NPC in that city\n            return \"character\", f\"{VAULT}/characters/npcs/minor\"\n        return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/cities\"\n\n    if \"dungeons/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"character\", f\"{VAULT}/characters/npcs/minor\"\n\n    if \"regions/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/regions\"\n\n    # Characters referenced from character pages\n    if \"characters/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"character\", f\"{VAULT}/characters/npcs/minor\"\n\n    # Chapters - usually NPCs or locations mentioned in story\n    if \"chapters/\" in ref_lower:\n        # Titles suggest NPC\n        titles = [\"lord\", \"lady\", \"king\", \"queen\", \"prince\", \"princess\", \"duke\",\n                  \"baron\", \"captain\", \"ambassador\", \"general\", \"professor\", \"doctor\",\n                  \"master\", \"sir\", \"dame\", \"father\", \"mother\", \"elder\"]\n        if any(name_lower.startswith(t + \" \") for t in titles):\n            return \"character\", f\"{VAULT}/characters/npcs/minor\"\n        if len(name_words) >= 2 and all(w[0].isupper() for w in name_words if w not in (\"the\", \"of\", \"and\", \"in\", \"on\", \"at\", \"to\", \"from\", \"with\", \"a\", \"an\")):\n            # Multi-word proper noun - could be character or location\n            # If it looks like a personal name (2-3 words, no \"the\")\n            if not name.startswith(\"The \") and len(name_words) <= 3:\n                return \"character\", f\"{VAULT}/characters/npcs/minor\"\n        return \"character\", f\"{VAULT}/characters/npcs/minor\"\n\n    # Lore references\n    if \"lore/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"concept\", f\"{VAULT}/lore\"\n\n    # Factions\n    if \"factions/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"faction\", f\"{VAULT}/factions\"\n\n    # Default: if it looks like a name, NPC; otherwise lore\n    if len(name_words) >= 2 and name_words[0][0].isupper():\n        return \"character\", f\"{VAULT}/characters/npcs/minor\"\n\n    return \"concept\", f\"{VAULT}/lore\"\n\n\ndef to_kebab(name):\n    \"\"\"Convert name to kebab-case filename.\"\"\"\n    # Remove apostrophes and special chars, replace spaces with hyphens\n    s = name.lower()\n    s = s.replace(\"'\", \"\").replace(\"'\", \"\")\n    s = re.sub(r'[^a-z0-9\\s-]', '', s)\n    s = re.sub(r'\\s+', '-', s.strip())\n    s = re.sub(r'-+', '-', s)\n    return s\n\n# ---- Process ----\nskipped_images = 0\nskipped_empty = 0\nskipped_generic = 0\ncreated = {\"character\": 0, \"location\": 0, \"item\": 0, \"faction\": 0, \"concept\": 0}\ncreated_files = []\nskipped_entries = []\n\nfor name, ref in entries:\n    # Skip empty/malformed\n    if is_empty_or_malformed(name):\n        skipped_empty += 1\n        continue\n\n    # Strip leading/trailing whitespace from name\n    name = name.strip()\n\n    # Skip images\n    if is_image(name):\n        skipped_images += 1\n        continue\n\n    # Skip generic D&D\n    if is_generic_dnd(name):\n        skipped_generic += 1\n        skipped_entries.append(f\"  GENERIC: {name}\")\n        continue\n\n    # Skip generic single-word locations\n    if is_generic_single_word_location(name, ref):\n        skipped_generic += 1\n        skipped_entries.append(f\"  GENERIC-LOC: {name}\")\n        continue\n\n    # Categorize\n    typ, directory = categorize(name, ref)\n\n    # Build filename\n    filename = to_kebab(name) + \".md\"\n    filepath = os.path.join(directory, filename)\n\n    # Skip if file already exists\n    if os.path.exists(filepath):\n        continue\n\n    # Determine source file for reference\n    # Strip .md from ref for wikilink\n    source = ref.replace(\".md\", \"\").split(\"/\")[-1]\n\n    # Create directory if needed\n    os.makedirs(directory, exist_ok=True)\n\n    # item\n    m = re.match(r\"(\\w+)\\s+of\\s+\", nl)\n    if m and m.group(1) in ITEM_KW:\n        return True\n    return False\n\ndef has_title(name):\n    first_word = name.lower().split()[0] if name.split() else \"\"\n    return first_word in TITLE_PREFIXES\n\ndef looks_like_person_name(name):\n    \"\"\"Heuristic: 2-3 words, each capitalized, no 'the', no location/faction keywords.\"\"\"\n    words = name.split()\n    if len(words) < 2 or len(words) > 4:\n        return False\n    if words[0].lower() == \"the\":\n        return False\n    # All significant words should be capitalized\n    for w in words:\n        if w.lower() in (\"the\", \"of\", \"and\", \"in\", \"on\", \"at\", \"to\", \"from\", \"with\", \"a\", \"an\", \"de\", \"von\", \"van\"):\n            continue\n        if not w[0].isupper():\n            return False\n    # Should NOT have location/faction/item keywords\n    if has_location_keyword(name) or has_faction_keyword(name) or has_item_keyword(name):\n        return False\n    return True\n\ndef is_single_word_proper(name):\n    \"\"\"Single capitalized word -- likely a place or unique noun.\"\"\"\n    words = name.split()\n    return len(words) == 1 and words[0][0].isupper()\n\n\ndef categorize(name, ref):\n    \"\"\"Determine type and output directory.\"\"\"\n    ref_lower = ref.lower()\n    name_lower = name.lower()\n\n    # 1. Check for item keywords first (specific)\n    if has_item_keyword(name):\n        return \"item\", f\"{VAULT}/lore\"\n\n    # 2. Check for faction keywords\n    if has_faction_keyword(name):\n        return \"faction\", f\"{VAULT}/factions\"\n\n    # 3. Check for titled NPCs (Lord X, Captain Y)\n    if has_title(name):\n        return \"character\", f\"{VAULT}/characters/npcs/minor\"\n\n    # 4. Check for location keywords\n    if has_location_keyword(name):\n        # Determine subdirectory from ref\n        if \"cities/\" in ref_lower:\n            return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/cities\"\n        if \"dungeons/\" in ref_lower:\n            return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/dungeons\"\n        if \"planes/\" in ref_lower:\n            return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/planes\"\n        if \"regions/\" in ref_lower:\n            return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/regions\"\n        return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations\"\n\n    # 5. Person-name heuristic\n    if looks_like_person_name(name):\n        return \"character\", f\"{VAULT}/characters/npcs/minor\"\n\n    # 6. \"The X\" pattern -- usually location or faction or concept\n    if name.startswith(\"The \"):\n        if \"locations/\" in ref_lower or \"cities/\" in ref_lower:\n            return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations\"\n        if \"factions/\" in ref_lower:\n            return \"faction\", f\"{VAULT}/factions\"\n        return \"concept\", f\"{VAULT}/lore\"\n\n    # 7. Referenced from planes\n    if \"planes/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/planes\"\n\n    # 8. Referenced from factions\n    if \"factions/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"faction\", f\"{VAULT}/factions\"\n\n    # 9. Remaining multi-word from specific locations\n    if \"cities/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/cities\"\n    if \"dungeons/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/dungeons\"\n    if \"regions/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/regions\"\n    if \"locations/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"character\", f\"{VAULT}/characters/npcs/minor\"\n    if \"lore/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"concept\", f\"{VAULT}/lore\"\n\n    return \"concept\", f\"{VAULT}/lore\"\n\n\ndef to_kebab(name):\n '-', s)\n    return s\n\n\n# ---- Process ----\nskipped_images = 0\nskipped_empty = 0\nskipped_generic = 0\ncreated = {\"character\": 0, \"location\": 0, \"item\": 0, \"faction\": 0, \"concept\": skipped_generic += 1\n        continue\n\n    typ, directory = categorize(name, ref)\n\n    filename = to_kebab(name) + \".md\"\n    filepath = os.path.join(directory, filename)\n\n    if {"parentUuid":"c2a22ea4-39f2-400b-91fd-2da959a5203b","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ae7ca5a3fc49dc061","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01SJcuzdts2dErNd8MGBSirP","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_016yxgxMQrjWstSnXBWo2nSt","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/tmp/create-stubs-v3.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Parse orphan-stubs-needed.md, filter, categorize, and create stub pages. v3 final.\"\"\"\n\nimport os\nimport re\n\nVAULT = \"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager/mardov-vault\"\n\n# ---- Parse ----\nentries = []\nwith open(f\"{VAULT}/meta/orphan-stubs-needed.md\") as f:\n    for line in f:\n        m = re.match(r'^- \\*\\*(.*?)\\*\\* -- referenced in: (.*)$', line.strip())\n        if m:\n            entries.append((m.group(1).strip(), m.group(2).strip()))\n\nprint(f\"Total entries parsed: {len(entries)}\")\n\n# ---- Skips ----\nGENERIC_DND = {\n    \"Aasimar\", \"Bugbear\", \"Centaur\", \"Changeling\", \"Dhampir\", \"Dragonborn\",\n    \"Drow\", \"Dwarf\", \"Eladrin\", \"Elf\", \"Firbolg\", \"Genasi\", \"Githyanki\",\n    \"Githzerai\", \"Gnoll\", \"Gnome\", \"Goblin\", \"Goliath\", \"Grung\", \"Halfling\",\n    \"Half-Elf\", \"Half-Orc\", \"Harengon\", \"Hexblood\", \"Hobgoblin\", \"Human\",\n    \"Kalashtar\", \"Kenku\", \"Kobold\", \"Leonin\", \"Lizardfolk\", \"Loxodon\",\n    \"Minotaur\", \"Orc\", \"Owlin\", \"Reborn\", \"Satyr\", \"Shifter\", \"Tabaxi\",\n    \"Tiefling\", \"Tortle\", \"Triton\", \"Vedalken\", \"Warforged\", \"Yuan-Ti\",\n    \"Abolith\", \"Aboleth\", \"Abby Devil\", \"Abishai\", \"Ankheg\", \"Beholder\",\n    \"Druid\", \"Paladin\", \"Warlock\", \"Wizard\", \"Sorcerer\", \"Cleric\", \"Ranger\",\n    \"Barbarian\", \"Bard\", \"Fighter\", \"Monk\", \"Rogue\", \"Artificer\",\n}\n\nGENERIC_SINGLE_WORDS = {\"Desert\", \"Harbor\", \"Underground\", \"Volcano\", \"Forest\",\n    \"Mountain\", \"River\", \"Ocean\", \"Cave\", \"Temple\", \"Castle\", \"Tower\", \"Village\",\n    \"City\", \"Town\", \"Swamp\", \"Plains\", \"Ruins\", \"Dungeon\", \"Tavern\",\n    \"Market\", \"Arena\", \"Library\", \"Prison\", \"Graveyard\", \"Mine\"}\n\nIMAGE_EXTS = {'.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.gif', '.webp', '.webm', '.svg', '.bmp', '.tiff', '.mp4', '.mov'}\n\n# ---- Location/faction/item keyword sets ----\nLOCATION_KW = set(\"\"\"tower keep hall gate port district quarter ward square bridge market\ntavern inn temple shrine cathedral fortress citadel palace castle dungeon cave mine pit\ngrove lake river mountain island bay harbor reef apothecary shop emporium bazaar arena\ncolosseum library academy university school guildhall barracks prison graveyard cemetery\ndocks wharf lighthouse observatory workshop foundry forge sanctum vault crypt tomb ruins\nabbey monastery cafe bakery brewery distillery cottage condominiums pits glade heights\nrow lane road street alley way path trail pass crossing falls springs well den lair nest\ncamp outpost garrison watchtower rampart wall chamber room cellar attic rooftop balcony\nplaza garden park courtyard terrace pier jetty marina shipyard drydock church chapel altar\nestate manor mansion hut shack crater ravine canyon gorge chasm abyss plateau mesa cliff\nridge swamp marsh bog fen moor wasteland tundra steppe savanna jungle thicket copse dell\nsea strait channel lagoon spire pinnacle peak summit sanctuary haven refuge retreat asylum\noffice study parlor salon lounge stairs corridor passage tunnel gallery canderie flat\nthrone seat ward wing\"\"\".split())\n\nFACTION_KW = set(\"\"\"guild order brotherhood sisterhood council alliance clan tribe syndicate\ncartel legion army circle coven cult cloaks guard watch patrol band company regiment\nbattalion corps pact union society league federation collective assembly coalition\nconsortium cabal enclave\"\"\".split())\n\n# \"house\" and \"court\" only count as faction if preceded by a proper noun\nFACTION_KW_CONTEXTUAL = {\"house\", \"court\"}\n\nITEM_KW = set(\"\"\"sword blade staff wand amulet necklace crown helm shield armor cloak boots\ngauntlets potion scroll tome gem crystal orb rod scepter axe bow dagger locket lantern\nhorn flute lyre pendant key diadem mask chalice goblet coin token relic talisman charm\nidol banner standard sigil seal\"\"\".split())\n# Note: \"ring\" omitted (too many false positives), \"hammer\" omitted (dwarf surname),\n# \"heart\" omitted (too ambiguous), \"anchor\" omitted (campaign concept), \"stone\" omitted,\n# \"book\" omitted, \"mark\" omitted\n\nTITLE_PREFIXES = set(\"\"\"lord lady king queen prince princess duke duchess baron baroness\ncount countess captain sergeant commander chief professor doctor master mistress sir dame\nfather mother brother sister elder ambassador general governor mayor sheriff warden priest\npriestess oracle seer sage chancellor magistrate judge marshal commodore admiral lieutenant\ncorporal squire knight\"\"\".split())\n\n# ---- Helpers ----\ndef is_image(name):\n    return any(name.lower().endswith(ext) for ext in IMAGE_EXTS)\n\ndef is_empty_or_malformed(name):\n    return not name or len(name.strip()) <= 1\n\ndef is_generic(name):\n    return name in GENERIC_DND or name in GENERIC_SINGLE_WORDS\n\ndef name_has_keyword(name, keywords, contextual=None):\n    nl = name.lower()\n    words = nl.split()\n    last_word = words[-1] if words else \"\"\n    if last_word in keywords:\n        return True\n    # Check contextual keywords (need preceding proper noun)\n    if contextual:\n        if last_word in contextual and len(words) >= 2:\n            return True\n    # Check \"'s X\" pattern\n    m = re.search(r\"['']s?\\s+(\\w+)$\", nl)\n    if m and m.group(1) in keywords:\n        return True\n    return False\n\ndef has_title(name):\n    first_word = name.lower().split()[0] if name.split() else \"\"\n    return first_word in TITLE_PREFIXES\n\ndef looks_like_person_name(name):\n    \"\"\"2-4 words, each significant word capitalized, no location/faction/item keywords.\"\"\"\n    words = name.split()\n    if len(words) < 2 or len(words) > 4:\n        return False\n    if words[0].lower() == \"the\":\n        return False\n    filler = {\"the\", \"of\", \"and\", \"in\", \"on\", \"at\", \"to\", \"from\", \"with\", \"a\", \"an\", \"de\", \"von\", \"van\", \"la\", \"le\"}\n    for w in words:\n        if w.lower() in filler:\n            continue\n        if not w[0].isupper():\n            return False\n    if name_has_keyword(name, LOCATION_KW):\n        return False\n    if name_has_keyword(name, FACTION_KW, FACTION_KW_CONTEXTUAL):\n        return False\n    if name_has_keyword(name, ITEM_KW):\n        return False\n    return True\n\n\ndef categorize(name, ref):\n    ref_lower = ref.lower()\n\n    # --- Rule 0: npc-catalog entries are ALWAYS characters ---\n    if \"npc-catalog\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"character\", f\"{VAULT}/characters/npcs/minor\"\n\n    # --- Rule 0b: location-catalog entries are locations ---\n    if \"location-catalog\" in ref_lower:\n        if name_has_keyword(name, FACTION_KW, FACTION_KW_CONTEXTUAL):\n            return \"faction\", f\"{VAULT}/factions\"\n        return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations\"\n\n    # 1. Item keywords\n    if name_has_keyword(name, ITEM_KW):\n        return \"item\", f\"{VAULT}/lore\"\n\n    # 2. Faction keywords\n    if name_has_keyword(name, FACTION_KW, FACTION_KW_CONTEXTUAL):\n        return \"faction\", f\"{VAULT}/factions\"\n\n    # 3. Titled NPCs\n    if has_title(name):\n        return \"character\", f\"{VAULT}/characters/npcs/minor\"\n\n    # 4. Location keywords\n    if name_has_keyword(name, LOCATION_KW):\n        if \"cities/\" in ref_lower:\n            return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/cities\"\n        if \"dungeons/\" in ref_lower:\n            return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/dungeons\"\n        if \"planes/\" in ref_lower:\n            return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/planes\"\n        if \"regions/\" in ref_lower:\n            return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/regions\"\n        return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations\"\n\n    # 5. Person-name heuristic (2+ capitalized words, no keywords)\n    if looks_like_person_name(name):\n        return \"character\", f\"{VAULT}/characters/npcs/minor\"\n\n    # 6. \"The X\" patterns\n    if name.startswith(\"The \"):\n        if \"locations/\" in ref_lower or \"cities/\" in ref_lower:\n            return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations\"\n        if \"factions/\" in ref_lower:\n            return \"faction\", f\"{VAULT}/factions\"\n        return \"concept\", f\"{VAULT}/lore\"\n\n    # 7. Reference-path fallbacks\n    if \"planes/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/planes\"\n    if \"factions/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"faction\", f\"{VAULT}/factions\"\n\n    # 8. Single-word proper nouns\n    words = name.split()\n    if len(words) == 1 and words[0][0].isupper():\n        if \"locations/\" in ref_lower or \"cities/\" in ref_lower:\n            return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations\"\n        if \"characters/\" in ref_lower:\n            return \"character\", f\"{VAULT}/characters/npcs/minor\"\n        if \"chapters/\" in ref_lower:\n            return \"character\", f\"{VAULT}/characters/npcs/minor\"\n        return \"concept\", f\"{VAULT}/lore\"\n\n    # 9. Remaining by ref path\n    if \"cities/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/cities\"\n    if \"dungeons/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/dungeons\"\n    if \"regions/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations/regions\"\n    if \"locations/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"location\", f\"{VAULT}/locations\"\n    if \"characters/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"character\", f\"{VAULT}/characters/npcs/minor\"\n    if \"chapters/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"character\", f\"{VAULT}/characters/npcs/minor\"\n    if \"lore/\" in ref_lower:\n        return \"concept\", f\"{VAULT}/lore\"\n\n    return \"concept\", f\"{VAULT}/lore\"\n\n\ndef to_kebab(name):\n    s = name.lower()\n    s = s.replace(\"'\", \"\").replace(\"\\u2019\", \"\").replace(\"\\u2018\", \"\")\n    s = re.sub(r'[^a-z0-9\\s-]', '', s)\n    s = re.sub(r'\\s+', '-', s.strip())\n    s = re.sub(r'-+', '-', s)\n    return s\n\n\ndef clean_name_for_stub(name):\n    \"\"\"For catalog entries like 'Name - Description', extract just the name.\"\"\"\n    if \" - \" in name:\n        return name.split(\" - \")[0].strip()\n    return name\n\n\n# ---- Process ----\nskipped = {\"images\": 0, \"empty\": 0, \"generic\": 0}\ncreated = {\"character\": 0, \"location\": 0, \"item\": 0, \"faction\": 0, \"concept\": 0}\nalready_exists = 0\ncreated_files = []\n\nfor raw_name, ref in entries:\n    if is_empty_or_malformed(raw_name):\n        skipped[\"empty\"] += 1\n        continue\n\n    raw_name = raw_name.strip()\n\n    if is_image(raw_name):\n        skipped[\"images\"] += 1\n        continue\n\n    if is_generic(raw_name):\n        skipped[\"generic\"] += 1\n        continue\n\n    # Categorize using full name (including description after dash)\n    typ, directory = categorize(raw_name, ref)\n\n    # For filename and stub title, use cleaned name\n    display_name = clean_name_for_stub(raw_name)\n    filename = to_kebab(display_name) + \".md\"\n    filepath = os.path.join(directory, filename)\n\n    if os.path.exists(filepath):\n        already_exists += 1\n        continue\n\n    source = ref.replace(\".md\", \"\").split(\"/\")[-1]\n\n    os.makedirs(directory, exist_ok=True)\n\n    content = f\"\"\"---\ntype: {typ}\nname: \"{display_name}\"\nstatus: stub\n---\n\n# {display_name}\n\n*Stub page -- referenced in [[{source}]]. Needs content.*\n\"\"\"\n\n    with open(filepath, 'w') as f:\n        f.write(content)\n\n    created[typ] += 1\n    created_files.append((filepath.replace(VAULT + \"/\", \"\"), typ, display_name))\n\n# ---- Report ----\nprint(f\"\\n=== RESULTS ===\")\nprint(f\"Skipped (images):      {skipped['images']}\")\nprint(f\"Skipped (empty):       {skipped['empty']}\")\nprint(f\"Skipped (generic D&D): {skipped['generic']}\")\nprint(f\"Already existed:       {already_exists}\")\nprint(f\"\\nStubs created by category:\")\ntotal = 0\nfor cat in [\"character\", \"location\", \"faction\", \"item\", \"concept\"]:\n    count = created[cat]\n    print(f\"  {cat:12s}: {count}\")\n    total += count\nprint(f\"  {'TOTAL':12s}: {total}\")\n\n# Show samples\nprint(f\"\\n=== SAMPLES ===\")\nfor cat in [\"character\", \"location\", \"faction\", \"item\", \"concept\"]:\n    samples = [f for f in created_files if f[1] == cat][:5]\n    if samples:\n        print(f\"\\n  {cat}:\")\n        for path, _, name in samples:\n            print(f\"    {name:40s} -> {path}\")\n\n# Verify key edge cases\nprint(f\"\\n=== EDGE CASE CHECKS ===\")\nchecks = [\n    (\"Alfren Goodfellow\", \"character\"),\n    (\"Borris -
